transition-colors inline-block" href="/help">← Back</a><div class="prose prose-lg max-w-none"><h1 class="text-large mb-8">Grok Bot FAQs</h1>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Short answers for access, usage, service status, and using Grok Bot. Longer steps live on the linked articles.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription" data-heading-base-id="do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription" aria-label="Copy link to do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Do I need a separate Grok Bot subscription?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. Grok Bot comes with <strong>Cursor Pro</strong>, <strong>Pro+</strong>, <strong>Ultra</strong>, and self-serve <strong>Teams</strong>. You can also link an individual <strong>SuperGrok</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Plus</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Heavy</strong>, or <strong>X Premium+</strong> account. On Enterprise, ask your account team. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/plans#how-do-i-get-access-to-grok-bot">Plans and billing</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="is-grok-bot-the-same-as-the-grok-app"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#is-grok-bot-the-same-as-the-grok-app" data-heading-base-id="is-grok-bot-the-same-as-the-grok-app" aria-label="Copy link to is-grok-bot-the-same-as-the-grok-app"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Is Grok Bot the same as the Grok app?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. Grok (the chat app from xAI) and Grok Bot are two different apps. A paywall or a <strong>Weekly SuperGrok Limit</strong> in the Grok app is not about Grok Bot. Grok Bot signs in with your Cursor account. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/sign-in">Sign in to Grok Bot</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="which-grok-plans-can-i-link"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#which-grok-plans-can-i-link" data-heading-base-id="which-grok-plans-can-i-link" aria-label="Copy link to which-grok-plans-can-i-link"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Which Grok plans can I link?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Individual <strong>SuperGrok</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Plus</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Heavy</strong>, and <strong>X Premium+</strong>. <strong>SuperGrok Lite</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Team</strong>, and <strong>SuperGrok Enterprise</strong> cannot link. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok#which-supergrok-tiers-grant-grok-bot">Which SuperGrok tiers grant Grok Bot?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="i-dont-see-link-grok-account"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#i-dont-see-link-grok-account" data-heading-base-id="i-dont-see-link-grok-account" aria-label="Copy link to i-dont-see-link-grok-account"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">I don&#x27;t see Link Grok Account.</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">That option shows only on the <strong>Get Started</strong> screen, before you sign in. Settings does not have it. Fully quit Grok Bot (on Mac, choose <strong>Quit</strong> from the menu bar), then open it again. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok#why-dont-i-see-a-link-grok-account-option-after-ive-already-logged-in">Why don&#x27;t I see a Link Grok Account option after I&#x27;ve already logged in?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="i-linked-supergrok-to-the-wrong-cursor-account-can-i-unlink-it"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#i-linked-supergrok-to-the-wrong-cursor-account-can-i-unlink-it" data-heading-base-id="i-linked-supergrok-to-the-wrong-cursor-account-can-i-unlink-it" aria-label="Copy link to i-linked-supergrok-to-the-wrong-cursor-account-can-i-unlink-it"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">I linked SuperGrok to the wrong Cursor account. Can I unlink it?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. A link is permanent, and you cannot move it yourself. <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help">Contact support</a> with the account that got the link and the account that should have it. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok#can-i-unlink-or-move-a-supergrok-link-to-a-different-cursor-account">Can I unlink or move a SuperGrok link?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="can-i-change-the-email-on-my-account"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#can-i-change-the-email-on-my-account" data-heading-base-id="can-i-change-the-email-on-my-account" aria-label="Copy link to can-i-change-the-email-on-my-account"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Can I change the email on my account?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. The email on a Cursor account cannot change. To use a different email, make a new account. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/change-email">Change your email address</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="im-stuck-in-a-sign-in-loop-or-see-an-authentication-error"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#im-stuck-in-a-sign-in-loop-or-see-an-authentication-error" data-heading-base-id="im-stuck-in-a-sign-in-loop-or-see-an-authentication-error" aria-label="Copy link to im-stuck-in-a-sign-in-loop-or-see-an-authentication-error"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">I&#x27;m stuck in a sign-in loop or see an authentication error.</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Update the app, then sign out and sign in again. Clear cookies for cursor.com and x.ai and try a private window. If the phone loops, sign in on desktop first. If the error is new, wait 30 to 60 minutes. Reinstalling usually does not help. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/sign-in#why-is-grok-bot-showing-a-persistent-authentication-error-even-after-i-log-out-clear-cache-and-reinstall">Sign in to Grok Bot</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="is-grok-bot-down"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#is-grok-bot-down" data-heading-base-id="is-grok-bot-down" aria-label="Copy link to is-grok-bot-down"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Is Grok Bot down?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Check <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://status.cursor.com">status.cursor.com</a> and look at the <strong>Grok Bot</strong> row. The app doesn&#x27;t show service status, so the status page is the place to check. You can subscribe to updates there.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If the status page shows a Grok Bot incident:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Wait for the incident to be resolved. The status page posts updates.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Don&#x27;t <strong>Reset</strong> the computer or reinstall the app. Neither fixes a service problem, and <strong>Reset</strong> can lose very recent changes if the computer can&#x27;t be reached to save them.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">When the incident is resolved, send your message again.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If the status page shows no incident and the Bot says <strong>Bot failed to respond</strong> or <strong>Model provider is overloaded</strong>, wait a moment and send the message again. If the Bot still doesn&#x27;t reply, fully quit and reopen Grok Bot, then follow <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/how-to#how-do-i-troubleshoot-grok-bot-computer-routine-connector-and-startup-problems">How do I troubleshoot Grok Bot computer, routine, connector, and startup problems?</a> If it keeps happening, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a> with the request ID.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="my-bot-stopped-replying-and-says-i-hit-my-usage-limit-is-it-broken"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#my-bot-stopped-replying-and-says-i-hit-my-usage-limit-is-it-broken" data-heading-base-id="my-bot-stopped-replying-and-says-i-hit-my-usage-limit-is-it-broken" aria-label="Copy link to my-bot-stopped-replying-and-says-i-hit-my-usage-limit-is-it-broken"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">My Bot stopped replying and says I hit my usage limit. Is it broken?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. Your weekly usage is used up. It resets each week. To keep going now, turn on on-demand usage or move to a bigger Cursor plan. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/plans#how-does-grok-bot-usage-work">How does Grok Bot usage work?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="why-doesnt-my-supergrok-limit-match-my-grok-bot-usage"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#why-doesnt-my-supergrok-limit-match-my-grok-bot-usage" data-heading-base-id="why-doesnt-my-supergrok-limit-match-my-grok-bot-usage" aria-label="Copy link to why-doesnt-my-supergrok-limit-match-my-grok-bot-usage"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Why doesn&#x27;t my SuperGrok limit match my Grok Bot usage?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">They are two separate meters for two products. Grok Bot usage lives on your Cursor account. See it in Grok Bot under <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Usage &amp; Billing</strong>, not in the Grok app. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/plans#what-do-the-plan-and-spending-screens-call-usage">What do the plan and spending screens call usage?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="do-a-cursor-plan-and-supergrok-add-together"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#do-a-cursor-plan-and-supergrok-add-together" data-heading-base-id="do-a-cursor-plan-and-supergrok-add-together" aria-label="Copy link to do-a-cursor-plan-and-supergrok-add-together"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Do a Cursor plan and SuperGrok add together?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. They do not stack. For more usage, upgrade your Cursor plan (for example <strong>Pro+</strong> to <strong>Ultra</strong>) or turn on on-demand usage. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/plans#do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack">Do Cursor and SuperGrok plans stack?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-stop-charges-after-my-weekly-usage-runs-out"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-stop-charges-after-my-weekly-usage-runs-out" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-stop-charges-after-my-weekly-usage-runs-out" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-stop-charges-after-my-weekly-usage-runs-out"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I stop charges after my weekly usage runs out?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Turn off on-demand, or set a cap. In Grok Bot, open <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>On-demand monthly limit</strong>. On the web, go to <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://cursor.com/dashboard">cursor.com/dashboard</a> &gt; <strong>Spending</strong> &gt; <strong>Monthly Limit</strong>. The cap does not stop a Bot in the middle of a run, so a running task can go a little past it. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/plans#how-does-the-on-demand-monthly-limit-apply-to-grok-bot">How does the on-demand monthly limit apply to Grok Bot?</a> and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/overages">Usage-based charges</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="my-trial-ran-out-in-one-task-can-i-get-it-back"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#my-trial-ran-out-in-one-task-can-i-get-it-back" data-heading-base-id="my-trial-ran-out-in-one-task-can-i-get-it-back" aria-label="Copy link to my-trial-ran-out-in-one-task-can-i-get-it-back"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">My trial ran out in one task. Can I get it back?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. The trial is a usage credit, and one big task can use all of it. Used credit is not restored. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/plans#what-if-my-trial-ran-out-during-a-single-run">What if my trial ran out during a single run?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="i-paid-in-the-iphone-or-android-app-how-do-i-cancel-or-get-a-refund"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#i-paid-in-the-iphone-or-android-app-how-do-i-cancel-or-get-a-refund" data-heading-base-id="i-paid-in-the-iphone-or-android-app-how-do-i-cancel-or-get-a-refund" aria-label="Copy link to i-paid-in-the-iphone-or-android-app-how-do-i-cancel-or-get-a-refund"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">I paid in the iPhone or Android app. How do I cancel or get a refund?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Apple or Google handles it. Cancel in <strong>Apple ID</strong> &gt; <strong>Subscriptions</strong>, or in <strong>Google Play</strong> &gt; <strong>Payments &amp; subscriptions</strong>. Cursor cannot cancel or refund store purchases. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/app-store-subscription#how-do-i-manage-an-in-app-subscription">Subscribe from the iOS app</a>, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/google-play-subscription#how-do-i-manage-an-in-app-subscription">Subscribe from the Android app</a>, and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/refunds">Refunds</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="do-i-have-to-pay-again-on-my-phone"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#do-i-have-to-pay-again-on-my-phone" data-heading-base-id="do-i-have-to-pay-again-on-my-phone" aria-label="Copy link to do-i-have-to-pay-again-on-my-phone"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Do I have to pay again on my phone?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. Sign in to the phone app with the same Cursor account, and your access carries over. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/mobile#do-i-need-to-buy-again-on-mobile-if-my-cursor-account-already-includes-grok-bot">Do I need to buy again on mobile?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="does-my-bot-keep-working-when-my-laptop-is-closed"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#does-my-bot-keep-working-when-my-laptop-is-closed" data-heading-base-id="does-my-bot-keep-working-when-my-laptop-is-closed" aria-label="Copy link to does-my-bot-keep-working-when-my-laptop-is-closed"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Does my Bot keep working when my laptop is closed?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Yes. Bots work on a cloud computer. Closing the app, the laptop, or the phone does not stop a task or a routine. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/routines">Routines</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="does-the-bot-use-my-own-computer"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#does-the-bot-use-my-own-computer" data-heading-base-id="does-the-bot-use-my-own-computer" aria-label="Copy link to does-the-bot-use-my-own-computer"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Does the Bot use my own computer?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Bots work on their own cloud computer. A Bot runs a command on your desktop only if you allow it. The setting is under <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Computer</strong>, labeled <strong>Execution on this computer</strong>. If no desktop is listed there yet, look under <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>General</strong> &gt; <strong>Bot</strong> for <strong>Execution on Local Computer</strong>. By default it asks you each time. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/grok-bot/settings">Grok Bot settings</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="why-does-a-website-block-my-bot-or-show-a-captcha"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#why-does-a-website-block-my-bot-or-show-a-captcha" data-heading-base-id="why-does-a-website-block-my-bot-or-show-a-captcha" aria-label="Copy link to why-does-a-website-block-my-bot-or-show-a-captcha"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Why does a website block my Bot or show a captcha?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Some sites block traffic from cloud computers. When that happens, the Bot hands you the step, and you can sign in yourself on the Bot&#x27;s computer. You can also turn on <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Computer</strong> &gt; <strong>Route traffic through this computer</strong>, so sites see your own connection.</p>

<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="my-chats-or-bots-are-missing"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#my-chats-or-bots-are-missing" data-heading-base-id="my-chats-or-bots-are-missing" aria-label="Copy link to my-chats-or-bots-are-missing"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">My chats or Bots are missing.</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">They are usually not deleted. Fully quit and reopen. Check another device on the same account. Open <strong>Hidden Bots</strong>. Make sure you are on the same Cursor account. Don&#x27;t use <strong>Recover</strong> or <strong>Reset</strong> to look for them. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/computer-recovery#my-chats-or-bots-look-missing-were-they-deleted">My chats or bots look missing</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="the-computer-says-reconnecting-or-couldnt-reach-grok-bots-computer"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#the-computer-says-reconnecting-or-couldnt-reach-grok-bots-computer" data-heading-base-id="the-computer-says-reconnecting-or-couldnt-reach-grok-bots-computer" aria-label="Copy link to the-computer-says-reconnecting-or-couldnt-reach-grok-bots-computer"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">The computer says Reconnecting or &quot;Couldn&#x27;t Reach Grok Bot&#x27;s Computer.&quot;</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Wait and choose <strong>Retry</strong>. Then fully quit and reopen. Then <strong>Update</strong>, then <strong>Recover</strong>. Use <strong>Reset</strong> only last. Like <strong>Recover</strong>, it can lose very recent changes if the computer can&#x27;t be reached to save them. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/computer-recovery#which-should-i-try-first">Which should I try first?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="what-does-the-low-disk-space-warning-mean"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#what-does-the-low-disk-space-warning-mean" data-heading-base-id="what-does-the-low-disk-space-warning-mean" aria-label="Copy link to what-does-the-low-disk-space-warning-mean"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What does the low disk space warning mean?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Computer is low on disk space</strong> or <strong>Computer is critically low on disk space</strong> is about your Bots&#x27; cloud computer, not your laptop. Choose <strong>Go to Disk Saver</strong>. Disk Saver is a Bot that checks what is using space and suggests what to clean up. It asks before it deletes anything, and nothing is deleted unless you confirm. Your chats are not affected. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/computer-recovery#my-chats-or-bots-look-missing-were-they-deleted">My chats or bots look missing</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="why-did-an-approval-expire-before-i-saw-it"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#why-did-an-approval-expire-before-i-saw-it" data-heading-base-id="why-did-an-approval-expire-before-i-saw-it" aria-label="Copy link to why-did-an-approval-expire-before-i-saw-it"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Why did an approval expire before I saw it?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Approvals from work that started without you, like a routine or a message from another Bot, expire after about 10 minutes. Approvals in a chat you are in wait for you. Turn on the Bot&#x27;s <strong>Notifications</strong>, or choose <strong>Always allow</strong> for actions you trust. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/how-to#why-did-an-approval-expire">Why did an approval expire?</a></p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="can-i-pick-the-model"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#can-i-pick-the-model" data-heading-base-id="can-i-pick-the-model" aria-label="Copy link to can-i-pick-the-model"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Can I pick the model?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. Cursor picks the model for the specific task under the hood. There is no model picker in Grok Bot. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/grok-bot/settings">Grok Bot settings</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="which-devices-work"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#which-devices-work" data-heading-base-id="which-devices-work" aria-label="Copy link to which-devices-work"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Which devices work?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The desktop app runs on Mac, Windows, and Linux. The phone app runs on iPhone and Android. Get the desktop app from the Grok Bot page at <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://cursor.com/dashboard/bot">cursor.com/dashboard/bot</a>. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/mobile">Grok Bot on mobile</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-contact-support"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-contact-support" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-contact-support" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-contact-support"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I contact support?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help">Contact support</a> while signed in to your Cursor account. Include your account email, your Grok Bot version (account menu &gt; <strong>About</strong> &gt; <strong>Copy version info</strong>), a screenshot, and the request ID (right-click the message &gt; <strong>Copy request ID</strong>). See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/how-to#how-do-i-find-my-grok-bot-version">How do I find my Grok Bot version?</a> and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/how-to#how-do-i-copy-a-request-id">How do I copy a request ID?</a>.</p>
